# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a03 — INV-6 reset of dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a02 with the verdict and audit-pool split (PR #410; plan 024 P1-4a)

This text is the independent redesign by a review-profile context other than the author of a02. Plan 006 INV-6 S2 stopped a02 with amendments 1, questions 1 and revises 1. Its revise-1 head `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff` received two further P1 and three P2. Seven local-review P1 across three heads establish that the combined three-script, three-schema surface requires a split.

PR #410 continues on `tools/verdict-audit-pool-a01` from `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff`. The worker's first push deletes `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a02/task.md`, adds the dispatched text's byte-identical copy at `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a03/task.md`, removes `scripts/orchestrator/audit-pool.sh`, `.schemas/classes.yaml` and `tests/unit/test_audit_pool.py` from the branch, and restores `scripts/orchestrator/orch-audit.sh` to `origin/main`. These named deletions and restoration are the explicit split bookkeeping exception to the worker's edit boundary. Subsequent implementation edits remain within `allowed_files`.

The orchestrator preserves historical branch evidence with its original task identifiers and tested heads, and removes predecessor artifacts from the cumulative PR diff as reset bookkeeping. The final cumulative inventory contains only this task's allowed files. The a01 and a02 acceptance histories retain their stopped status and original counters.

The sibling `dotfiles-T133-audit-pool-a01` owns the pool, audit wrapper, class vocabulary and pool tests on its own branch and PR. This task creates no implementation branch or PR. PR C stage 1 remains parked as draft PR #407 at `dfa9001b858431751faeab3b779b71eb0da01c2f`, and the completed parking step is not repeated.

The four premises preserve a02's write-time observations verbatim. Their references to the former combined items remain historical. The design is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-programs.md` items (1) and (3), with the nine conditions in `.orchestration/plans/024-design-mechanism.md`. Programs items (2) and (16) remain outside this task.

## Goal

The orchestrator obtains one schema-valid verdict from nine positively checked acceptance conditions. `--apply` reaches neither acceptance dispatch nor merge without a successful integration gate for the accepted task, head and PR. Later cap amendments govern condition 5.

The verdict implementation, both JSON schemas and the verdict tests form one independently reviewable PR. The retained interpreter, finding-count, feedback and loop-refresh fixes remain enforced.

## Files

1. `scripts/orchestrator/verdict.sh <pr> [--apply] [--loop] [--shadow]` remains Bash with `set -Eeuo pipefail`, English shdoc documentation and `jq` for JSON. Every condition is `{id, ok, evidence}` and becomes true only through a positive check of parsed, validated input.

   Inputs are `gh pr view <pr> --json headRefOid,isDraft,baseRefName,state,number`, `gh pr checks <pr> --json name,state`, the acceptance record whose JSON block names the PR, task and current head and contains a ledger array, its prose `precheck: <sha256>`, the selected task or `--task <id>`, `.orchestration/validation/<task>-audit-<sha7>.json`, a fresh `scripts/pr-feedback.py <pr> --json <mktemp>` sweep, the saved `.orchestration/validation/<task>-pr-feedback.json` dispositions, the task's `allowed_files`, `security` and Check caps, regime counters, the merge-base diff and numstat, and the required head-specific security review. The PR is open, non-draft and based on `main`. A missing head is fetched from `pull/<pr>/head` and must resolve locally before evaluation continues.

   Condition 1 verifies that `git show <head>:.orchestration/<task>/precheck.json` hashes to the record's `precheck:` value and names the head or an ancestor whose diff to the head contains only `.orchestration/<task>/` paths. Unset or zero `PRECHECK_REQUIRED` makes failure of this condition a warning.

   Condition 2 requires every required check to be present with state `SUCCESS` for the head. Empty or unreadable checks hold. Every duplicate entry for a required name must also be `SUCCESS`.

   Condition 3 requires an audit that validates against `.schemas/audit.json`, names this task and head, has zero open P0/P1 findings, and has verdict `correct` or a non-empty set of remaining findings whose scopes are all `records`. A blocked audit holds. An incorrect audit without remaining findings fails this condition.

   Condition 4 requires both saved and fresh feedback documents to name the accepted head, complete feedback identity matching with multiplicity, and a valid disposition for every fresh item. Diagnostic URLs do not determine the result.

   Condition 5 requires every path in `git diff --name-only <base> <head>` to match an `allowed_files` entry and additions-plus-deletions totals from `git diff --numstat --no-renames <base> <head>` to remain within the effective Check caps. The base is `git merge-base origin/main <head>`. An unreadable base or diff holds. Backticked exclusions after `outside` leave the outside count. Default exclusions are `tests/` and `.orchestration/`. Item 7 defines amendment precedence.

   Condition 6 runs `scripts/regime-counters.sh <task> --pr <pr>` with its ledger source. No `S1:` line is present and the parsed `revises` value is below 2. A missing ledger, failed command or unreadable count holds.

   Condition 7 requires the numeric count of open P0/P1 findings to be zero. Findings with status `new`, `still-open` or absent count as open regardless of identifier or scope.

   Condition 8 requires `.orchestration/validation/<task>-security-<sha7>.md.last.md` to end with the last non-blank line `Verdict: correct` when the task's `security` is true. Unreadable front matter cannot select the non-security path.

   Condition 9 requires at least one `sha256:<hex> <path>` line in the acceptance record's prose and verifies every such line against its file.

   All nine conditions passing produces `accept`. Failure of condition 6 or 7, missing or unreadable input, schema-invalid input or an internal evaluation error produces `hold`. Other failed conditions produce `revise`. The revise line remains `AGMSG-ACCEPTANCE v1 task_id=<task> status=revise previous_head=<head> reason=<failed ids> next_action=<the first failed condition's evidence, shell-safe>`. The orchestrator supplies `check=` selectors.

   The output is `.orchestration/validation/<task>-verdict-<sha7>.json`, validated against `.schemas/verdict.json`, with `verdict`, `pr`, `head`, `task_id`, `conditions[]`, `inputs_sha256{}`, `generated_at` and optional `revise_line`. Exit codes remain 0 for accept, 10 for revise, 20 for hold and 2 for usage or internal error, with an internal error treated as hold. Refusal before a usable task or head exists reports hold without inventing an output identity.

   `--apply` requires the pinned execution context, tracked scripts, schemas and task files equal to `origin/main`, and no untracked executable helper substituted in those code directories. The last four rows of `.orchestration/validation/verdict-shadow.tsv` must agree between machine and human and represent four distinct PRs. Four rows for one PR do not unlock application. Item 6 adds the integration gate before either application effect.

   After accept and a successful gate, application writes `AGMSG-ACCEPTANCE v1 task_id=<task> status=accepted reason=verdict-<sha7> next_action=merge` to a temporary file, sends it through `scripts/orchestrator/dispatch.sh <team> <from> <to> <pane> <file> <record>`, and invokes `gh pr merge <pr> --squash --match-head-commit <head>`. Team, sender, recipient and placement come from `--team`, `--from`, `--to` and `--pane`. Arguments containing `--admin` or `--auto` anywhere are refused with exit 2 before any read or tool invocation.

   `--shadow` writes the verdict JSON and appends `<pr>\t<head>\t<verdict>\t\t\t<generated_at>` to `.orchestration/validation/verdict-shadow.tsv`, with columns `pr`, `head`, `machine`, `human`, `agree` and `at`. The orchestrator supplies the human and agreement columns. Shadow mode sends nothing and merges nothing.

   `--loop` processes eligible open, non-draft PRs on `main` in PR-number order when an acceptance record exists and names the PR's current head. It retains the pinned context, fetches origin between PRs, and with `--apply` fast-forwards the trusted checkout to `origin/main` after each child. Failure to fast-forward holds with exit 20. Fetching alone does not satisfy the retained refresh requirement.

2. `.schemas/verdict.json` and `.schemas/audit.json` remain JSON Schema 2020-12 documents with `additionalProperties: false` at the top level and in defined item objects. Their fields retain the inherited verdict and audit contracts.

   The audit record contains `head`, `task_id`, `auditor`, `generated_at`, `verdict` and findings with `id`, `priority`, `confidence`, `file`, `line`, `class`, `scope`, `rationale` and optional `status`. The controlled class enum retains the vocabulary snapshot at `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff`. Its absence as a separate classes file in this branch does not loosen that enum.

   Schema validation remains available inside this task's verdict implementation under its pinned isolated interpreter. The split removes the runtime dependency on the sibling's new `orch-audit.sh --validate` mode. The existing stdlib schema-checking behavior is retained without a new helper file or dependency. Required keys, types, enums, bounds, SHA patterns, additional-property restrictions and RFC 3339 date-times with an offset remain checked. Date-only values, naive timestamps and invalid calendar dates fail.

   The sibling owns `.schemas/classes.yaml` and its equality test against this audit contract. This task changes neither the vocabulary file nor the pool implementation.

3. The interpreter-selection fix remains enforced. Before reading any PR checkout, the script resolves `uv`, Python, `gh`, `git`, `jq`, Bash and required shell utilities to absolute executables through the trusted-root, private-PATH pattern established by `scripts/orchestrator/orch-gate.sh`. Shim refusal remains enforced and the environment is explicitly constructed.

   Python runs through the pinned absolute interpreter with isolation. The offline PyYAML environment is resolved and pinned before PR input processing. Subsequent helpers, schema checks, the feedback collector, task-front-matter reading and the gate invocation retain the same tool and interpreter boundary. Checkout `.python-version`, `.venv`, executable files, uv configuration, mise configuration and inherited tool-selection variables cannot select another interpreter or tool. Missing trusted tools or unavailable offline dependencies hold before dispatch or merge.

   `test_verdict_pins_tools_interpreter_and_environment_before_reading_a_pr_checkout` retains its planted marker-writing interpreter and checkout-local and inherited-selector cases. Its historical red evidence against `85a472294d0c10835bd7235e3c76d045846a6ed9` remains identified as historical. The final-head green result proves that pinned evaluation works and the planted interpreter never writes its marker.

4. The remaining a02 fixes remain plain acceptance requirements.

   Conditions 3 and 7 count open P0/P1 findings numerically. Empty identifiers cannot hide a finding and records scope cannot exempt it. `test_verdict_counts_open_p0_p1_findings_even_when_the_id_is_empty` retains P0, P1, `new`, `still-open` and fixed controls, with refreshed audit hashes.

   Condition 4 requires the saved and fresh `head_sha` values to equal the accepted head. `test_verdict_condition_4_requires_saved_and_fresh_heads_to_equal_the_accepted_head` retains stale and missing-head cases for both documents.

   Condition 4 counts missing or invalid dispositions before formatting URLs. Null and empty URLs affect diagnostics only. `test_verdict_condition_4_rejects_an_undispositioned_status_with_a_null_url` retains its rejected status and valid-disposition control.

   Complete feedback identity and multiplicity use the trusted root's existing `feedback_key()` and `missing_feedback()` behavior from `scripts/require-crit-review.py`. Source, URL, level, path, line, body and author participate in identity, with only the existing exact masked-body and masked-path equivalence accepted. Each saved occurrence covers at most one fresh occurrence. `test_verdict_condition_4_preserves_complete_item_identity_and_multiplicity` retains shared-URL annotations, duplicate fresh identities with too few saved occurrences, and one valid saved occurrence per fresh occurrence.

   Disposition validation reuses `parse_disposition()` and `commit_in_range()` from the trusted root's existing `scripts/require-crit-review.py` without modifying it. A fix commit exists inside the authenticated base-to-head range. A not-applicable disposition has a non-empty reason and evidence command and satisfies existing level-specific reason requirements. The report and RESULT identify head, base, branch, changed files, every feedback disposition, test names, code and test totals, schema counts, task-copy hash and evidence paths. A Codex seat writes evidence at the same relative paths in its own worktree and states that placement in the RESULT. Worker review evidence uses the required `-worker-` infix and the orchestrator copies worktree evidence by absolute path before review.

This task is `security: true`. The orchestrator runs its own final-head task-level audit and separate security-profile review before the gate. Final-head CI, the feedback sweep, finding dispositions, acceptance record, integration gate and merge follow Orchestrator Playbook step 10 in its prescribed order. Changed heads receive fresh required evidence. The orchestrator merges with `gh pr merge --squash --match-head-commit <audited head>` after the gate. Four-distinct-PR shadow enforcement remains in code and this PR is integrated manually.

No `@codex review` comment is posted. Automatic review is observed for the bounded window and absence after 15 minutes is recorded as `bot: none`. Commands matching a live ask rule are not issued and no API or browser path bypasses that rule. Each `gh` command, Git write and `git fetch` runs individually without shell chaining or a wrapper combining commands. Refused commands retain their command, exit status and output while independent authorized work continues.

Until plan 024 P1-14 lands, every string containing the words `gh pr merge` or `gh pr create` is written with the file-edit tool. Shell commands, heredocs and shell wrappers do not write those strings. This writing rule supplies no authorization to execute a command refused by the live permission boundary.

Live dispatch, merge and local Bats are not exercised. A trusted gate that rejects the head argument keeps live application on hold.

.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:189:The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. gh keys Keychain credentials by host and user, so a worker running as the same macOS user can read the orchestrator's token regardless of `GH_CONFIG_DIR`. Prevention of a worker-initiated merge requires a separate OS user or host under D-024-23's future P1-16, which the operator decides whether to pursue. Until then the GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. The regime detects missing acceptance or gate-passage evidence in the next records PR, not at main-push time, because the records reach main only with that boundary PR. On a PR touching `.orchestration/acceptance/**`, the regime job checks every main merge commit since the previous boundary for its corresponding acceptance record with gate-passage evidence and fails the PR when one is missing. P1-11 owns implementation of that check; this task states section 5.14 item 0's detection rule. Approvals and gate evidence of record are read from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage without establishing isolation from a malicious same-UID process.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:191:This task implements section 5.14 items 1, 9 and 10 through a dedicated Claude worker configuration directory, static Edit deny rules on trusted paths and worktree configuration, managed sandbox enforcement, guarded project hooks, hook interpreter and PATH bindings, private worker caches and the rule that worker commands run inside their sandbox. Every Claude seat has `sandbox.failIfUnavailable: true`.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:197:The design source is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:201:> 5.14 item 1: Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。 hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。 `failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:203:> 5.14 item 2: 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:205:> 5.14 item 9: orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。 Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).

.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:217:The file-tool rule is the documented sentence, “Deny rules: apply when either the requested path or the file it resolves to matches.” Edit and Write refuse a requested file that is itself a symlink. .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:445:    `tests/unit/test_claude_managed_settings.py::ClaudeManagedSettingsTest::test_rendered_hooks_bind_executables_and_permgate_returns_decision` renders the complete orchestrator and worker user hook sets and the guard in a temporary HOME, reusing the tracked and launcher-generated hook fixtures for SessionStart, Stop and delivery. .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:469:The worker's before-amendment-4 measurements are 674 changed source lines, guard included, and 905 changed test lines (2026-10-19T08:21:22Z evidence at local head 55c125a1 on main 69dccca9); re-measuring that worker diff, including its pending validator/exclusion edits, gives the same totals. Estimate 20 additional changed test lines for the new inventory assertions, 45 for the replacement-policy test, 35 for the planted-user-site extension of the hook-execution test and 12 for the isolated-token fixture/assertion adjustments: `1190 + 20 + 45 + 35 + 12 = 1302`. The source cap therefore rises from 874 to 888 and the test cap from 1190 to 1302, only to these estimated sums. .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:485:The permission guard and merge gate remain P1-14 a05's work. P1-14 a05 and P1-6 implement the regime rule that approvals and gate evidence of record come from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. The GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:487:For the committed approval ledger, the regime obtains the commit SHA returned by `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` and reads `gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>` inside the sandbox. The consuming implementation reads the API response's file content at that exact SHA. It does not fall back to local files, local refs or local object traversal.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:489:A working-copy decision remains a proposal until its record PR reaches main. This task writes no approval row and implements no local-object substitute for GitHub authority. Shared credentials can still authorize a worker-initiated merge. P1-11 owns the next-records-PR regime check for missing acceptance or gate evidence under section 5.14 item 0's detection rule.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:499:The documented file-tool checks reduce symlink redirection risk. A same-UID process can race filesystem changes, alter host-local state or execute outside a seat sandbox. The remaining interval between permission checks and path consumption is a residual under section 5.14 item 0. This text makes no claim that the worker could already write every redirected target directly.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:507:In `scripts/validate-agent-assets.py`, the consolidated policy-check changes enumerated in item 16 are in scope; unrelated validator behavior remains out of scope. In `tests/unit/test_validate_agent_assets.py`, the two exclusion cases, `valid_claude_sandbox`, the native-binding render fixture, the named hook fixture and structural-hook cases in item 16 are in scope; unrelated tests remain out of scope. The named literal-form assertions and fixtures in the already allowed generator and merge test modules are included as item 16 specifies.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:523:The validation record contains exact commands, verbatim outputs, exit statuses and tested identities. Git commands producing that evidence follow section 5.14 item 2.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:531:Hook evidence inventories every generated and tracked entry, its `onFailure` value, `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME="$HOME" LANG=C.UTF-8 TERM=dumb CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}" CLAUDE_ENV_FILE="${CLAUDE_ENV_FILE}"` prefix (with template-rendered absolute HOME for PermissionRequest), installed script, absolute entrypoint interpreter and reached descendants. It includes the new render/runtime selector's actual exit status and parsed permgate decision under `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin`, plus first-word and post-env-assignment executable checks for every rendered hook command. It includes the exact `run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <abs python> python -I` sequence, the planted `.pth` and `usercustomize.py`, the fixture control's markers and their absence during the isolated real-hook invocation. It states the portable tracked-command and per-host-rendered-binding split, verifies HOME re-export before HOME-relative paths and shows that tracked commands name no interpreter or anchor path. It includes worker user settings and main-checkout local settings. Render evidence shows that `ruff`, `prettier` and `terminal-browser` resolve under the guard's rendered orchestrator tool PATH on the rendering host and that no PATH entry is a shims directory. Merged-settings evidence identifies runtime-added entries separately. No report claims complete effective failure-policy coverage from template inspection alone.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:559:Every gh write and every git write runs as a separate command without shell chaining. Every worker command remains inside its sandbox. An operation that cannot complete there produces a blocked PONG with the exact command and boundary.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:27:    disposition: "residual: tests/unit/test_trusted_interpreter.py::TrustedInterpreterTest::test_pinned_interpreter_ignores_checkout_path_without_mise is the positive case; its red state is the refusal path (before the implementation the resolver does not exist, so the test fails by absence); a named missing-installation skip is not positive coverage; install-symlink and install-not-regular cannot be exercised in CI or by the worker (implemented, refuses by code, verified by reading); temporary refusal fixtures and read-only real-installation checks do not establish host directory protection"

.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:68:  - control: design 5.14 item 0 same-UID threat model
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:97:`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 applies **by reference**, including items 1–10 and the same-UID threat model. Under C-88, the following verbatim sentences are normative; the design path supplies context rather than replacing these requirements.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:99:Item 2: “A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.” “Python は `-I`。” “PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。”
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:111:Before traps, tool discovery or declaration reads, verify the actual environment's exact allowed names through `/usr/bin/env` and `/usr/bin/grep`, the fixed PATH and LANG values, the script's absolute realpath and the passwd-home match. Account explicitly for Bash's own maintained environment entries so the clean prefix succeeds; allow no sanitization sentinel or production test exemption. Require `$0` to be the bound absolute canonical script pathname and its realpath to be a regular file under the trusted repository root, outside worker roots; refuse a symlinked `$0`, including a worker-owned symlink to the trusted script, without reopening it. Any invalid environment, script path or HOME exits 2 with a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic and no stdout, instead of repairing or re-executing.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:115:Obtain the real uid through `/usr/bin/id -ru`. On macOS obtain its user name through `/usr/bin/id -run` and its passwd home through `/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory`; on Linux use `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>`. Refuse a missing, ambiguous or malformed passwd result, or any disagreement between that passwd home and HOME, with exit 2 and a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic before selecting a candidate. Derive the installation root only as `<passwd home>/.local/share/mise/installs`, never from the HOME variable, and construct only `<install root>/python/<version>/bin/python3`. Resolve and validate the candidate before printing it. Require an executable regular file after `realpath`, within the expected protected installation, outside the worktree, manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and `$TMPDIR`. Check resolved path boundaries, including filesystem case behavior where applicable. Refuse a symlinked installation rather than accepting a redirected install directory.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:117:On success print exactly one absolute interpreter realpath and exit 0. On every refusal print exactly one line `trusted-interpreter: refused: <code>` to stderr, print no interpreter on stdout, and exit 2. The codes are `dirty-environment`, `script-path`, `home-mismatch`, `declaration-missing`, `declaration-malformed`, `declaration-duplicate`, `install-missing`, `install-not-regular`, `install-symlink`, `install-outside-root` and `version-mismatch`. Invalid entry environment uses `dirty-environment`; a noncanonical or symlinked script path uses `script-path`; invalid passwd data or HOME disagreement uses `home-mismatch`. An absent declaration uses `declaration-missing`, duplicates use `declaration-duplicate`, and malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations use `declaration-malformed`. An absent candidate uses `install-missing`, a non-executable or non-regular candidate uses `install-not-regular`, a symlinked installation uses `install-symlink`, a forbidden resolved path uses `install-outside-root`, and a resolved installation version differing from the pin uses `version-mismatch`. Every negative test asserts its specific reason code as well as exit 2 and no interpreter stdout; an earlier refusal is not coverage of a later check. There is no `command -v`, `mise which`, PATH search, version-alias selection, download or fallback. Do not execute a candidate to discover whether it is trustworthy.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:123:Apply the same trusted-parent clean prefix, privileged program entry, exact environment verification, canonical `$0` realpath check and real-uid passwd-home match to `scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh`. Refuse any entry failure with exit 2 and exactly one `ci-rerun: refused: <code>` line before tool setup or effects: `dirty-environment` for invalid environment names or values, `script-path` for a noncanonical or symlinked script path, and `home-mismatch` for invalid passwd data or HOME disagreement. Do not repair the environment or self re-exec. Invoke the resolver by its bound absolute realpath through the same clean prefix, supplying HOME from the verified passwd home, and consume its single validated result; propagate refusal without trying another interpreter.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:125:Remove Python from the old tool-discovery loop, including the line-45 fallback and line-47 shims test. Do not retain or recreate those mechanisms elsewhere. Bind non-Python tools from explicit protected installation locations, using their validated realpaths or a private fixed PATH of links to those realpaths; caller PATH entries never populate that directory. Missing trusted tools fail closed.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:127:Every external command, including cleanup on early failure and the environment, passwd-home and script-realpath checks, uses an absolute path or the fixed PATH established after entry verification. Keep the EXIT trap’s `/bin/rm` and cover both successful execution and refusal before tool setup completes. Apply the relevant section-5.14 Git invocation controls to the existing root-discovery probe; do not introduce new Git probes or arbitrary shell-command inputs.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:129:Entry verification must refuse any `GIT_*` name in the program's environment, including `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE` and `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`, before invoking Git. The trusted-parent clean prefix must remove them before exec. Confirm that the existing root-discovery probe returns the canonical trusted repository root bound to the script location; a repository-local `core.worktree` redirection must cause exit 2 with `ci-rerun: refused: repository-root`, rather than selecting another root or silently overriding the redirection. Confirm the root before deriving the registry, ledger or reservation paths.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:133:Before reading or writing the flake registry, ledger or reservation state, resolve `.github/flakes.yaml`, `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv` and the reservation parent `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d` with `realpath`. Require the registry and ledger to be regular files and the reservation parent to be a directory under the confirmed repository root, outside manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and `$TMPDIR`. Refuse symlinks in the destination or any path component below that root, and, on a case-insensitive filesystem, a destination differing from its declared pathname only by case. Use only the validated canonical destinations for subsequent reads and effects. Apply the same checks to an existing per-run reservation directory before inspecting its claim and to a newly claimed directory before using it. Missing output objects may be created only at the existing effect point after validating their existing parent chain and resolving the intended destination; resolve and type-check each newly created object before appending ledger data or using a claim. Preserve atomic per-run creation and never replace an existing object during validation. Any destination-validation failure exits 2 with exactly one `ci-rerun: refused: <code>` line before registry parsing, ledger access or a rerun, leaving redirected targets unchanged. Destination codes are `destination-outside-root` for a resolved path outside the confirmed repository or in a forbidden root, `destination-symlink` for an otherwise contained path with a symlink in the destination or its parent chain, `destination-case-alias` for an otherwise contained case alias, `destination-not-regular` for an invalid registry or ledger file type, and `destination-not-directory` for an invalid reservation directory type. Check containment before symlink, case-alias and type checks so an outside-root mutation has its own distinguishable code.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:145:Write the failing regressions before the fix and paste red and green output. Every negative security case asserts its specific refusal code, not just exit 2 or an empty marker. Declaration-mutating negative cases (missing, malformed, duplicate, non-exact and metacharacter-bearing declarations, and the exact declaration used for a missing installation) execute the canonical resolver in a temporary copy of the repository tree through the trusted-parent clean prefix. Mutate only that copy's declaration; keep the real uid's passwd-home installation read-only and retain the real passwd-home lookup and install-root anchor. Add `tests/unit/test_trusted_interpreter.py` with class `TrustedInterpreterTest` and these tests:
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:147:- `test_pinned_interpreter_ignores_checkout_path_without_mise`: prepend a temporary checkout’s marker-writing `.venv/bin/python3` to PATH with mise unavailable; direct dirty entry exits 2 with `trusted-interpreter: refused: dirty-environment` and no marker. Its positive clean-prefix case invokes the canonical resolver in the real repository, reads that repository’s pinned declaration and the tool-manager-installed interpreter beneath the real uid’s passwd-home install root read-only, and expects exactly one absolute realpath and exit 0. Assert that the printed path is an executable regular file under that install root, then execute it as `"<abs>" -I -c 'import sys; print(sys.version_info[:2])'` and compare the output with the pinned version’s major/minor pair. Assert the resolver exists before considering a skip: its red state is the refusal path, failing by absence before implementation. If the pinned installation is absent, skip only this positive case with the named reason `pinned-installation-unavailable`, including the pin and expected install root, and record the skip in `report.md` and `validation.md`; do not skip dirty-entry or other negative cases. An existing installation that fails validation or version matching is a test failure, not a skip; the marker remains absent in every case.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:150:- `test_duplicate_malformed_nonexact_or_metacharacter_declarations_are_refused`: through the clean prefix in the temporary repository copy, cover duplicate declarations with `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-duplicate`; malformed grammar, non-exact versions including `latest`, prefix or suffix lookalikes, and metacharacter-bearing values each assert `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-malformed`. Every case exits 2 with no interpreter stdout and no marker.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:152:- `test_program_entry_refuses_dirty_environment_or_symlinked_script`: invoke the program with exported functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `LD_PRELOAD` and `LD_AUDIT` naming a marker-library path, planted PATH, a forged sanitization sentinel and a symlinked `$0`; dirty-entry cases assert `trusted-interpreter: refused: dirty-environment` and the symlinked-script case asserts `trusted-interpreter: refused: script-path`, each with exit 2, no interpreter stdout and no marker. The trusted-parent clean-prefix success assertion uses the read-only positive case above, with the real repository’s canonical script path and no marker. For direct dirty-entry refusal tests use a non-loadable marker-library path; a loadable library can execute before the script, so test its absence only with the trusted parent's pre-exec clean environment, never as a shell-entry guarantee.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:153:- `test_forged_home_is_refused`: set HOME to a forged directory containing a marker-writing interpreter at the expected mise layout, retaining an otherwise allowed environment; assert exit 2 with `trusted-interpreter: refused: home-mismatch`, no interpreter stdout and no marker before that interpreter can execute. The clean-prefix success assertion with the real uid's passwd home is the read-only positive case above.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:155:`install-symlink` and `install-not-regular` cannot be exercised in CI or by the worker (implemented, refuses by code, verified by reading). Their threat-table row remains `residual`, not `closed`. Do not manufacture symlinked, non-regular, forbidden-root or case-aliased installed candidates under the real passwd home, redirect its install root, or count an earlier refusal as runtime coverage of these checks.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:159:Retain and adapt `test_ci_rerun_starts_the_pinned_interpreter_by_its_own_path` and `test_ci_rerun_never_runs_a_path_resolved_command_before_the_pin`; their assertions must exercise the new trusted-path contract rather than preserve acceptance of arbitrary PATH wrappers or the removed shims mechanism. Add `test_ci_rerun_executes_as_a_sanitized_program` for exported functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `LD_PRELOAD` and `LD_AUDIT` naming a marker-library path, planted PATH, the forged-sentinel case, forged HOME and a worker-owned symlinked `$0`; assert `ci-rerun: refused: dirty-environment` for hostile environment inputs, `ci-rerun: refused: home-mismatch` for forged HOME and `ci-rerun: refused: script-path` for symlinked `$0`, each with exit 2, no marker and no rerun. Apply the same non-loadable-path limitation to direct dirty-loader refusal cases; with a loadable marker library, only the trusted-parent clean-prefix case asserts no marker. Clean-prefix execution in a CI-provisioned successful consumer fixture with the canonical script path and real passwd home must still use the pinned interpreter for both Python invocation sites and permit the expected rerun.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:161:Add these tests to `tests/unit/test_ci_rerun.py::CiRerunTest`. Each destination test starts from an accepted baseline: a temporary copy of the repository tree whose own `scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh` is executed as the program through the trusted-parent clean prefix. The copy must be the program's confirmed repository root, with its registry, ledger and reservation paths accepted; make one positive assertion of exit 0 and the expected fixture-only behavior first. The CI runner provisions these temporary copies outside the forbidden worker/cache/tmp roots, using the real pinned installation read-only; the sandboxed worker records blocked local evidence when that baseline is unavailable. Executing the original script against paths in another temporary tree is not an accepted baseline.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:163:For each independent negative invocation, restore the accepted baseline state and apply exactly one mutation: an otherwise in-root symlink at the destination or a parent (including a symlinked ledger and a symlinked reservation parent), a case-aliased in-root pathname (including the registry on a case-insensitive filesystem), or a parent redirected to a path outside the copy, including worker-writable cache and temporary destinations. Assert exactly `ci-rerun: refused: destination-symlink`, `ci-rerun: refused: destination-case-alias` or `ci-rerun: refused: destination-outside-root`, respectively, as well as exit 2, no registry parsing, no ledger append or reservation change, no rerun and an untouched target compared with its state immediately before the mutation. Keep symlink and case-alias subcases otherwise contained so an earlier containment refusal cannot satisfy them. Report an explicit skip for the case-alias subcase on a case-sensitive filesystem; do not claim that subcase ran.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:165:- `test_ci_rerun_refuses_unsafe_flake_registry_destination`: exercise all destination-refusal subcases for `.github/flakes.yaml`.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:166:- `test_ci_rerun_refuses_unsafe_ledger_destination`: exercise all destination-refusal subcases for `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv`, including a redirected ledger parent.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:167:- `test_ci_rerun_refuses_unsafe_reservation_destination`: exercise all destination-refusal subcases for `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d` and an existing per-run reservation, including a redirected reservation parent.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:168:- `test_ci_rerun_refuses_git_environment_inputs`: start with `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE` and `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL` pointing at planted locations in the outer environment, run through the trusted-parent clean prefix and assert that no `GIT_*` name survives at program entry, the confirmed root is the trusted fixture repository and the planted locations remain unused and unchanged. In separate subcases deliberately introduce each of those four names, plus another `GIT_*` name, after the prefix; the program must refuse at entry with exit 2 and `ci-rerun: refused: dirty-environment` before Git, registry reads, ledger or reservation access or any rerun.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:169:- `test_ci_rerun_refuses_repository_local_core_worktree`: set `core.worktree` in the fixture repository's local configuration to a planted worktree, invoke the consumer through the trusted-parent clean prefix with no `GIT_*` names and assert exit 2 at root confirmation with `ci-rerun: refused: repository-root`, before registry reads, ledger or reservation access or any rerun; the planted worktree remains unchanged.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:172:The positive resolver case reads the real repository and real installation read-only; it does not copy or rewrite the declaration, provision an installation, or create a repository outside the worker boundary. Every executable negative case, including forged HOME, malformed declaration, dirty environment, planted PATH and redirected destinations, uses temporary fixtures; declaration mutations use repository copies, and destination mutations require the accepted copied-repository baseline above. No negative case modifies the real passwd-home installation; symlinked and non-regular installation checks remain residual and are verified by reading. The passwd-home lookup always remains anchored to the real uid: do not substitute an account database, intercept that lookup, redirect the install root, or add a test seam that bypasses it.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:174:The inspected unit-test workflow `.github/workflows/test.yaml`, job `test`, has the existing mise setup step `Setup mise for statusline smoke` using `jdx/mise-action`, followed by `Install statusline tools` and later `Run Python unit tests`. The setup currently has `install: false`, and the listed tool-install commands do not install Python. Before dispatch, the orchestrator must arrange through a separate authorized prerequisite that this existing mise setup provisions the repository’s exact pinned Python under the runner’s real passwd-home mise install root before the Python tests. This task does not authorize workflow edits or worker writes beneath that root. CI runs the positive resolver case against its real checkout outside forbidden roots and its actual mise installation; it must record a passing case, not a missing-installation skip, for acceptance.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:176:Successful consumer fixture repositories outside worker/cache/tmp roots are created and exercised by the CI runner, using the same real pinned installation read-only; the sandboxed worker must not provision them or modify the real repository, installation or protected cache. Copy the resolver beside the consumer only in fixture repositories, supply fixture declarations there, and execute both scripts as programs through the trusted-parent clean prefix except for the explicit refusal cases. Obtain the successful consumer assertions’ red/green evidence in CI; record any locally unavailable fixture as blocked local evidence rather than claiming a pass or weakening the write boundary. Adapt fake-gh fixture data delivery to the clean environment; do not add production environment exemptions, executable-directory parameters or test-only resolver bypasses. Keep every existing behavioral assertion and keep all fixture calls off live GitHub.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:184:Test budget: the retained a02 test delta is `67 + 1 = 68` changed lines, plus about `130` for resolver tests including parser, forged-HOME, dirty-entry and reason-code cases, `110` for consumer regressions and clean-prefix fixture migration, and `90` for the three destination tests and two hostile-Git regressions, plus `20` for the foreign-cwd GitHub repository-binding regression, giving `68 + 130 + 110 + 90 + 20 = 418`, with `2` lines of allowance and a resulting cap of **420 changed lines under `tests/`**.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:190:Retain the predecessor’s checks: `make unit-test`, `make format`, ShellCheck and repository-style `shfmt -i 4 -sr -d` on the changed shell scripts, and the workflow-orchestration, workflow-security and ci-rerun unit modules. Add the trusted-interpreter module, the new startup and hostile-PATH cases, and the changed-file comparison lint. Do not run bats locally.
.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:220:Every `gh` command and every `git` write is an individual command **inside the sandbox**, including pushes and authenticated fetches. No compound write commands, outside-sandbox retries, excluded-command workaround, permission escalation or alternate tool to bypass a denial. Anything that cannot complete there produces `AGMSG-PONG v1 task_id=dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04 status=blocked` naming the exact command, failure and crossed boundary; preserve the evidence and wait.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:88:| 〃 | 権限・信頼境界を広げるタスク（excludedCommands、allowMachLookup、hooks で gh 書込を決める、sandbox の allowWrite） | **人** | 下の「権限の変更」に合流 | ユーザー |
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:99:## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:103:1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:104:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:105:3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:109:0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:110:8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder; a `gh api graphql` call binds the repository through fixed query variables (`owner: "mryfmo"`, `name: "dotfiles"` as constants in the script, passed with `-f`/`-F`) and uses only node ids obtained from a query bound by those variables (verdict a04 C-80 round 5, 2026-10-19): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:112:10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:113:7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

> 9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).


Host controls reduce accidents and prompt-injected damage. They do not isolate unrestricted processes sharing one UID. The design's GitHub prevention boundary is also not established while worker and orchestrator credentials remain accessible to that same UID; subsequent boundary-record checks provide detection as the design states.

## Branch and reset boundary

Continue `tools/verdict-audit-pool-a01`, PR #410, from `6b7e7833a85528440dc397c2555bc9dd603c685a`, already update-branched onto main `3f240b12`. Keep the a03 implementation, regression coverage and evidence. Create neither another implementation branch nor another PR.

The first push deletes `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a03/task.md` and adds the dispatched text byte-for-byte at `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a04/task.md`. This single predecessor-copy deletion is the explicit reset-bookkeeping exception to `allowed_files`. Other a03 evidence remains unchanged under its original task identifier and tested heads.

The orchestrator retains the a03 acceptance history, class rows, stopped status and counters. The final inventory identifies preserved a03 evidence separately from a04 additions; preservation does not authorize further worker edits in the a03 directory. The a01/a02 artifact removals already completed by a03 are not repeated or reversed.

The split remains intact. The pool sibling owns `scripts/orchestrator/audit-pool.sh`, changes to `scripts/orchestrator/orch-audit.sh`, `.schemas/classes.yaml` and `tests/unit/test_audit_pool.py`. `audit-pool.sh` is absent at `6b7e7833`; the audit schema at that head is `.schemas/audit.json`. Neither fact authorizes recreating a split file or adding a `schemas/` mirror. PR #407 remains parked; its parking operation is not repeated.

## Goal

Produce one schema-valid verdict from nine positively checked conditions without executing inherited shell startup code, accepting caller-selected executable directories, following output symlinks or treating invalid `security` metadata as an exemption.

Retain complete feedback validation, amended-cap precedence, offline operation and finding counts. This task computes and records shadow verdicts only; `--apply` is rejected as item 6 specifies.

## Files

1. `scripts/orchestrator/verdict.sh <pr> [--loop] [--shadow]` remains Bash with `set -Eeuo pipefail`, English shdoc documentation and `jq` for JSON. Its startup and executable selection follow the Reset design below. Each condition is `{id, ok, evidence}` and becomes true only through a positive check of parsed, validated input.

   Inputs remain the PR view and checks, the acceptance record's JSON block and prose, the selected task or `--task <id>`, the audit JSON, a fresh feedback sweep, saved feedback dispositions, the task's allowed paths and caps, regime counters, the GitHub merge-base changeset and the head-specific security review. The acceptance block names the PR, task and current head and contains a ledger array. The PR is open, non-draft and based on `main`. At the start of each PR evaluation, capture `head.sha` and `base.sha` together once from `gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/pulls/<pr>` as `head40` and `base40`, require both to be 40 lowercase hexadecimal characters, and bind all reads to that pair. Record both in the verdict JSON as `head` and `base`. The compare listing and file contents are read from GitHub at that captured pair; no separate current-main read replaces either value. If a second PR read is needed, compare both SHAs with the captured pair and refuse with hold, exit 20 and `verdict: PR base/head changed during evaluation` if either differs; append no shadow row and never combine reads from different pairs. This task chooses GitHub API reads, not a local clone: read the changeset through `…/compare/<base40>...<head40>` and inspected PR bytes through `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`. Repository identity is fixed to `mryfmo/dotfiles` from the trusted repository declaration, not caller environment or local Git configuration. Every `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` invocation supplies `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL. REST `gh api` calls, including instantiated REST templates in the design quotation, use an explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never `--repo` or a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder. `gh api graphql` calls instead carry `-f owner=mryfmo -f name=dotfiles` (or equivalent `-F` fields), supplied from fixed `owner: "mryfmo"` and `name: "dotfiles"` constants in the script, and use only thread node ids returned by the review-thread query bound by those variables. This REST/GraphQL distinction also governs the API endpoint wording in Completion. The retained fresh sweep invokes `scripts/pr-feedback.py`: its `THREADS_QUERY` supplies owner/name, but its `THREAD_COMMENTS_QUERY` currently supplies only id/cursor. Reuse its authenticated `collect("mryfmo/dotfiles", ...)` in the fixed isolated Python child with fixed-executable REST and GraphQL callbacks defined in `verdict.sh`; the GraphQL callback supplies both fixed repository fields on every request, including node pagination, and refuses an id not returned by the bound review-thread query. Neither helper is edited; `scripts/orchestrator/fill_sweep.py` only fills saved JSON dispositions and issues no `gh` calls. A matching head SHA in a fork is not a repository binding. Missing, incomplete, truncated or unreadable API data holds; no local-head fetch or shared-object fallback is allowed.

# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a03 — INV-6 reset of dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a02 with the verdict and audit-pool split (PR #410; plan 024 P1-4a)

This text is the independent redesign by a review-profile context other than the author of a02. Plan 006 INV-6 S2 stopped a02 with amendments 1, questions 1 and revises 1. Its revise-1 head `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff` received two further P1 and three P2. Seven local-review P1 across three heads establish that the combined three-script, three-schema surface requires a split.

PR #410 continues on `tools/verdict-audit-pool-a01` from `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff`. The worker's first push deletes `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a02/task.md`, adds the dispatched text's byte-identical copy at `.orchestration/dotfiles-T133-verdict-audit-pool-a03/task.md`, removes `scripts/orchestrator/audit-pool.sh`, `.schemas/classes.yaml` and `tests/unit/test_audit_pool.py` from the branch, and restores `scripts/orchestrator/orch-audit.sh` to `origin/main`. These named deletions and restoration are the explicit split bookkeeping exception to the worker's edit boundary. Subsequent implementation edits remain within `allowed_files`.

The orchestrator preserves historical branch evidence with its original task identifiers and tested heads, and removes predecessor artifacts from the cumulative PR diff as reset bookkeeping. The final cumulative inventory contains only this task's allowed files. The a01 and a02 acceptance histories retain their stopped status and original counters.

The sibling `dotfiles-T133-audit-pool-a01` owns the pool, audit wrapper, class vocabulary and pool tests on its own branch and PR. This task creates no implementation branch or PR. PR C stage 1 remains parked as draft PR #407 at `dfa9001b858431751faeab3b779b71eb0da01c2f`, and the completed parking step is not repeated.

The four premises preserve a02's write-time observations verbatim. Their references to the former combined items remain historical. The design is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-programs.md` items (1) and (3), with the nine conditions in `.orchestration/plans/024-design-mechanism.md`. Programs items (2) and (16) remain outside this task.

## Goal

The orchestrator obtains one schema-valid verdict from nine positively checked acceptance conditions. `--apply` reaches neither acceptance dispatch nor merge without a successful integration gate for the accepted task, head and PR. Later cap amendments govern condition 5.

The verdict implementation, both JSON schemas and the verdict tests form one independently reviewable PR. The retained interpreter, finding-count, feedback and loop-refresh fixes remain enforced.

## Files

1. `scripts/orchestrator/verdict.sh <pr> [--apply] [--loop] [--shadow]` remains Bash with `set -Eeuo pipefail`, English shdoc documentation and `jq` for JSON. Every condition is `{id, ok, evidence}` and becomes true only through a positive check of parsed, validated input.

   Inputs are `gh pr view <pr> --json headRefOid,isDraft,baseRefName,state,number`, `gh pr checks <pr> --json name,state`, the acceptance record whose JSON block names the PR, task and current head and contains a ledger array, its prose `precheck: <sha256>`, the selected task or `--task <id>`, `.orchestration/validation/<task>-audit-<sha7>.json`, a fresh `scripts/pr-feedback.py <pr> --json <mktemp>` sweep, the saved `.orchestration/validation/<task>-pr-feedback.json` dispositions, the task's `allowed_files`, `security` and Check caps, regime counters, the merge-base diff and numstat, and the required head-specific security review. The PR is open, non-draft and based on `main`. A missing head is fetched from `pull/<pr>/head` and must resolve locally before evaluation continues.

   Condition 1 verifies that `git show <head>:.orchestration/<task>/precheck.json` hashes to the record's `precheck:` value and names the head or an ancestor whose diff to the head contains only `.orchestration/<task>/` paths. Unset or zero `PRECHECK_REQUIRED` makes failure of this condition a warning.

   Condition 2 requires every required check to be present with state `SUCCESS` for the head. Empty or unreadable checks hold. Every duplicate entry for a required name must also be `SUCCESS`.

   Condition 3 requires an audit that validates against `.schemas/audit.json`, names this task and head, has zero open P0/P1 findings, and has verdict `correct` or a non-empty set of remaining findings whose scopes are all `records`. A blocked audit holds. An incorrect audit without remaining findings fails this condition.

   Condition 4 requires both saved and fresh feedback documents to name the accepted head, complete feedback identity matching with multiplicity, and a valid disposition for every fresh item. Diagnostic URLs do not determine the result.

   Condition 5 requires every path in `git diff --name-only <base> <head>` to match an `allowed_files` entry and additions-plus-deletions totals from `git diff --numstat --no-renames <base> <head>` to remain within the effective Check caps. The base is `git merge-base origin/main <head>`. An unreadable base or diff holds. Backticked exclusions after `outside` leave the outside count. Default exclusions are `tests/` and `.orchestration/`. Item 7 defines amendment precedence.

   Condition 6 runs `scripts/regime-counters.sh <task> --pr <pr>` with its ledger source. No `S1:` line is present and the parsed `revises` value is below 2. A missing ledger, failed command or unreadable count holds.

   Condition 7 requires the numeric count of open P0/P1 findings to be zero. Findings with status `new`, `still-open` or absent count as open regardless of identifier or scope.

**Checking schema validation tests**
