1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in 2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.

THREAD_COMMENTS_QUERY = """
query($id: ID!, $cursor: String) {
  node(id: $id) {
    ... on PullRequestReviewThread {
      comments(first: 100, after: $cursor) {
        pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor }
        nodes { databaseId }
      }
    }
  }
}
"""

Fetch = Callable[[str, bool], Any]
GraphQL = Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any]


def gh_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Environment for gh that never colours output, even under CLICOLOR_FORCE panes."""
    env = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if key not in {"CLICOLOR_FORCE", "GH_FORCE_TTY"}}
    env["NO_COLOR"] = "1"
    return env

99:Item 2: “A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.” “Python は `-I`。” “PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。”
105:Item 9: “orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。” “`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。” “One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19).”
109:Add executable `scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, with English shdoc comments and `#!/bin/bash -p`. Every caller executes it as a program through a trusted parent supplying `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …`; do not source it or use an unqualified `bash <file>` invocation. The parent itself must already be trusted and clean before starting `/usr/bin/env`; clearing the environment after a loader has consumed it is too late. There is no self re-exec.
111:Before traps, tool discovery or declaration reads, verify the actual environment's exact allowed names through `/usr/bin/env` and `/usr/bin/grep`, the fixed PATH and LANG values, the script's absolute realpath and the passwd-home match. Account explicitly for Bash's own maintained environment entries so the clean prefix succeeds; allow no sanitization sentinel or production test exemption. Require `$0` to be the bound absolute canonical script pathname and its realpath to be a regular file under the trusted repository root, outside worker roots; refuse a symlinked `$0`, including a worker-owned symlink to the trusted script, without reopening it. Any invalid environment, script path or HOME exits 2 with a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic and no stdout, instead of repairing or re-executing.
113:Read only the repository’s exact Python declaration established by the dispatch prerequisite, anchored to the trusted script/repository location rather than the caller’s cwd. Parse its fixed grammar as data with trusted absolute tools, without `source`, `eval`, shell interpolation of declaration content, or an interpreter selected from that content. Refuse missing, duplicate, malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations; `latest` is invalid.
115:Obtain the real uid through `/usr/bin/id -ru`. On macOS obtain its user name through `/usr/bin/id -run` and its passwd home through `/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory`; on Linux use `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>`. Refuse a missing, ambiguous or malformed passwd result, or any disagreement between that passwd home and HOME, with exit 2 and a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic before selecting a candidate. Derive the installation root only as `<passwd home>/.local/share/mise/installs`, never from the HOME variable, and construct only `<install root>/python/<version>/bin/python3`. Resolve and validate the candidate before printing it. Require an executable regular file after `realpath`, within the expected protected installation, outside the worktree, manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and `$TMPDIR`. Check resolved path boundaries, including filesystem case behavior where applicable. Refuse a symlinked installation rather than accepting a redirected install directory.
117:On success print exactly one absolute interpreter realpath and exit 0. On every refusal print exactly one line `trusted-interpreter: refused: <code>` to stderr, print no interpreter on stdout, and exit 2. The codes are `dirty-environment`, `script-path`, `home-mismatch`, `declaration-missing`, `declaration-malformed`, `declaration-duplicate`, `install-missing`, `install-not-regular`, `install-symlink`, `install-outside-root` and `version-mismatch`. Invalid entry environment uses `dirty-environment`; a noncanonical or symlinked script path uses `script-path`; invalid passwd data or HOME disagreement uses `home-mismatch`. An absent declaration uses `declaration-missing`, duplicates use `declaration-duplicate`, and malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations use `declaration-malformed`. An absent candidate uses `install-missing`, a non-executable or non-regular candidate uses `install-not-regular`, a symlinked installation uses `install-symlink`, a forbidden resolved path uses `install-outside-root`, and a resolved installation version differing from the pin uses `version-mismatch`. Every negative test asserts its specific reason code as well as exit 2 and no interpreter stdout; an earlier refusal is not coverage of a later check. There is no `command -v`, `mise which`, PATH search, version-alias selection, download or fallback. Do not execute a candidate to discover whether it is trustworthy.

Do not repair the environment or self re-exec. Invoke the resolver by its bound absolute realpath through the same clean prefix, supplying HOME from the verified passwd home, and consume its single validated result; propagate refusal without trying another interpreter.

Refuse symlinks in the destination or any path component below that root, and, on a case-insensitive filesystem, a destination differing from its declared pathname only by case. Use only the validated canonical destinations for subsequent reads and effects. Apply the same checks to an existing per-run reservation directory before inspecting its claim and to a newly claimed directory before using it. Destination codes are `destination-outside-root` for a resolved path outside the confirmed repository or in a forbidden root, `destination-symlink` for an otherwise contained path with a symlink in the destination or its parent chain, `destination-case-alias` for an otherwise contained case alias, `destination-not-regular` for an invalid registry or ledger file type, and `destination-not-directory` for an invalid reservation directory type.    Condition 8 requires `.orchestration/validation/<task>-security-<sha7>.md.last.md` to end with the last non-blank line `Verdict: correct` when the task's `security` is true. Unreadable front matter cannot select the non-security path.

   Condition 9 requires at least one `sha256:<hex> <path>` line in the acceptance record's prose and verifies every such line against its file.

   All nine conditions passing produces `accept`. Failure of condition 6 or 7, missing or unreadable input, schema-invalid input or an internal evaluation error produces `hold`. Other failed conditions produce `revise`. The revise line remains `AGMSG-ACCEPTANCE v1 task_id=<task> status=revise previous_head=<head> reason=<failed ids> next_action=<the first failed condition's evidence, shell-safe>`. The orchestrator supplies `check=` selectors.

   The output is `.orchestration/validation/<task>-verdict-<sha7>.json`, validated against `.schemas/verdict.json`, with `verdict`, `pr`, `head`, `task_id`, `conditions[]`, `inputs_sha256{}`, `generated_at` and optional `revise_line`. Exit codes remain 0 for accept, 10 for revise, 20 for hold and 2 for usage or internal error, with an internal error treated as hold. Refusal before a usable task or head exists reports hold without inventing an output identity.

   `--apply` requires the pinned execution context, tracked scripts, schemas and task files equal to `origin/main`, and no untracked executable helper substituted in those code directories. The last four rows of `.orchestration/validation/verdict-shadow.tsv` must agree between machine and human and represent four distinct PRs. Four rows for one PR do not unlock application. Item 6 adds the integration gate before either application effect.

   After accept and a successful gate, application writes `AGMSG-ACCEPTANCE v1 task_id=<task> status=accepted reason=verdict-<sha7> next_action=merge` to a temporary file, sends it through `scripts/orchestrator/dispatch.sh <team> <from> <to> <pane> <file> <record>`, and invokes `gh pr merge <pr> --squash --match-head-commit <head>`. Team, sender, recipient and placement come from `--team`, `--from`, `--to` and `--pane`. Arguments containing `--admin` or `--auto` anywhere are refused with exit 2 before any read or tool invocation.

   `--shadow` writes the verdict JSON and appends `<pr>\t<head>\t<verdict>\t\t\t<generated_at>` to `.orchestration/validation/verdict-shadow.tsv`, with columns `pr`, `head`, `machine`, `human`, `agree` and `at`. The orchestrator supplies the human and agreement columns. Shadow mode sends nothing and merges nothing.

   `--loop` processes eligible open, non-draft PRs on `main` in PR-number order when an acceptance record exists and names the PR's current head. It retains the pinned context, fetches origin between PRs, and with `--apply` fast-forwards the trusted checkout to `origin/main` after each child. Failure to fast-forward holds with exit 20. Fetching alone does not satisfy the retained refresh requirement.

2. `.schemas/verdict.json` and `.schemas/audit.json` remain JSON Schema 2020-12 documents with `additionalProperties: false` at the top level and in defined item objects. Their fields retain the inherited verdict and audit contracts.

   The audit record contains `head`, `task_id`, `auditor`, `generated_at`, `verdict` and findings with `id`, `priority`, `confidence`, `file`, `line`, `class`, `scope`, `rationale` and optional `status`. The controlled class enum retains the vocabulary snapshot at `b8998de162afc72d53d07679b8f0a194fcc480ff`. Its absence as a separate classes file in this branch does not loosen that enum.

   Schema validation remains available inside this task's verdict implementation under its pinned isolated interpreter. The split removes the runtime dependency on the sibling's new `orch-audit.sh --validate` mode. The existing stdlib schema-checking behavior is retained without a new helper file or dependency. Required keys, types, enums, bounds, SHA patterns, additional-property restrictions and RFC 3339 date-times with an offset remain checked. Date-only values, naive timestamps and invalid calendar dates fail.

   The sibling owns `.schemas/classes.yaml` and its equality test against this audit contract. This task changes neither the vocabulary file nor the pool implementation.

3. The interpreter-selection fix remains enforced. Before reading any PR checkout, the script resolves `uv`, Python, `gh`, `git`, `jq`, Bash and required shell utilities to absolute executables through the trusted-root, private-PATH pattern established by `scripts/orchestrator/orch-gate.sh`. Shim refusal remains enforced and the environment is explicitly constructed.

   Python runs through the pinned absolute interpreter with isolation. The offline PyYAML environment is resolved and pinned before PR input processing. Subsequent helpers, schema checks, the feedback collector, task-front-matter reading and the gate invocation retain the same tool and interpreter boundary. Checkout `.python-version`, `.venv`, executable files, uv configuration, mise configuration and inherited tool-selection variables cannot select another interpreter or tool. Missing trusted tools or unavailable offline dependencies hold before dispatch or merge.

   `test_verdict_pins_tools_interpreter_and_environment_before_reading_a_pr_checkout` retains its planted marker-writing interpreter and checkout-local and inherited-selector cases. Its historical red evidence against `85a472294d0c10835bd7235e3c76d045846a6ed9` remains identified as historical. The final-head green result proves that pinned evaluation works and the planted interpreter never writes its marker.

4. The remaining a02 fixes remain plain acceptance requirements.

   Conditions 3 and 7 count open P0/P1 findings numerically. Empty identifiers cannot hide a finding and records scope cannot exempt it. `test_verdict_counts_open_p0_p1_findings_even_when_the_id_is_empty` retains P0, P1, `new`, `still-open` and fixed controls, with refreshed audit hashes.

   Condition 4 requires the saved and fresh `head_sha` values to equal the accepted head. `test_verdict_condition_4_requires_saved_and_fresh_heads_to_equal_the_accepted_head` retains stale and missing-head cases for both documents.

   Complete feedback identity and multiplicity use the trusted root's existing `feedback_key()` and `missing_feedback()` behavior from `scripts/require-crit-review.py`. Source, URL, level, path, line, body and author participate in identity, with only the existing exact masked-body and masked-path equivalence accepted. Each saved occurrence covers at most one fresh occurrence. `test_verdict_condition_4_preserves_complete_item_identity_and_multiplicity` retains shared-URL annotations, duplicate fresh identities with too few saved occurrences, and one valid saved occurrence per fresh occurrence.

   Disposition validation reuses `parse_disposition()` and `commit_in_range()` from the trusted root's existing `scripts/require-crit-review.py` without modifying it. A fix commit exists inside the authenticated base-to-head range. A not-applicable disposition has a non-empty reason and evidence command and satisfies existing level-specific reason requirements. Evidence commands remain data and are never executed. `test_verdict_condition_4_uses_the_existing_disposition_and_commit_validators` retains empty and whitespace-only reasons, missing or repeated evidence delimiters, unknown commits, base commits, commits outside the head's ancestry, short failure reasons and valid positive controls.

   The historical red results for these retained fixes remain tied to their original heads. Their green results run again on the final implementation.

6. The b8998de P1 in the application path is fixed. After accept and the shadow unlock, `--apply` runs the trusted `scripts/orchestrator/orch-gate.sh <task> <head> <pr>` and requires its zero exit before any dispatch or merge. The parser honors the latest amendment's cap sentence, using the last applicable `at most <n>` declaration in text order instead of the first historical declaration. The effective sentence supplies its exclusions and test limit. A later test-only amendment replaces the test limit while retaining the outside limit.

   `test_verdict_condition_5_honors_the_latest_amended_caps` is written first. The report and RESULT identify head, base, branch, changed files, every feedback disposition, test names, code and test totals, schema counts, task-copy hash and evidence paths. This task is `security: true`. The orchestrator runs its own final-head task-level audit and separate security-profile review before the gate. Final-head CI, the feedback sweep, finding dispositions, acceptance record, integration gate and merge follow Orchestrator Playbook step 10 in its prescribed order. Changed heads receive fresh required evidence. The orchestrator merges with `gh pr merge --squash --match-head-commit <audited head>` after the gate. Four-distinct-PR shadow enforcement remains in code and this PR is integrated manually.

No `@codex review` comment is posted. Automatic review is observed for the bounded window and absence after 15 minutes is recorded as `bot: none`. Commands matching a live ask rule are not issued and no API or browser path bypasses that rule. Each `gh` command, Git write and `git fetch` runs individually without shell chaining or a wrapper combining commands. Refused commands retain their command, exit status and output while independent authorized work continues.

Until plan 024 P1-14 lands, every string containing the words `gh pr merge` or `gh pr create` is written with the file-edit tool. Shell commands, heredocs and shell wrappers do not write those strings. This writing rule supplies no authorization to execute a command refused by the live permission boundary.
